Bold Suitor is the pick on proven Lingfield AW seven-furlong credentials: he won this exact trip here on 30 April (beaten only 1L in second here two weeks earlier) with CD 1-2 and solid distance form on AW. OR 71 keeps him near the top of the weights without the fragility of Red Hat Eagle’s exposed mark or the unknowns around Dancing Rinca and Powder Monkey; draw 9 sits in the better half of Lingfield’s high-stall lean, and Georgia Dobie’s recent Lingfield winner gives a small positive on the saddle. I am not treating the blanket 1.1 quotes as ability rankings—this looks like an open Class 5 with no single dominant market horse.

The main threat is Split Elevens, who arrives off a course-and-distance win six days ago (1/13 @ 6.0), holds strong Lingfield figures (C 3-6, CD 2-5), and maps ideally to stall 13 under the historical high-draw bias. John Butler also runs Gunfighter from 10, which complicates pace but could still leave Split Elevens as the yard’s in-form leg; if Split Elevens has escaped a punishing re-handicap off that latest win, Bold Suitor’s edge narrows sharply despite the superior official rating on paper.

Azuinthejungle is the pick on form and setup, not market: three straight wins (Thirsk, Beverley, Ayr) point to a horse still ahead of a 72 mark in a field where most are 79–84. Draw 9 fits Newcastle’s high-draw lean (~44% of winners from high stalls over six years), and Nigel Tinkler’s second string Duran looks stale and out of form by comparison. Little Mi Mi is the main danger — 3–7 course-and-distance, 3–9 at Newcastle, and a June AW win here — but draw 3 works against the same bias that helps Azuinthejungle; Buzz Box (3–9 course, Jason Hart) and Lethal Nymph (draw 11, 1–4 CD) are next in the mix.

The biggest risk is surface and class: all three recent wins came on good/good-firm turf at Class 5, whereas this is Class 4 on standard AW, so the lenient OR may finally be tested. If Smart Vision or Castan inject a strong pace and Copper Knight leads from draw 2, a hold-up profile could find this trickier than the raw form line suggests.
